Hall of flowers

Herbarium schrodlmensis

Every post and note on this site grows one flower from its name, and the same name always grows the same flower. Here they all hang together, each with its label.

⡏⢉⠍⡩⢉⠍⡩⢉⠍⡩⢉⠍⡩⢉⢹⡇⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢸⡇⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⢸⡇⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⢸⡇⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢸⡇⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⢸⡇⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⢸⡇⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢸⡇⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⢸⡇⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⢸⡇⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢸⡇⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⡡⢊⠔⢸⣇⣊⣐⣁⣊⣐⣁⣊⣐⣁⣊⣐⣁⣊⣸
